More remarkably, its flexibility extends to several nontrivial scenarios: it enables pure pruning training, supports discrete weights, accommodates unconventional transfer functions such as Gaussian, and reveals the substantial redundancy of deep networks. We have demonstrated its feasibility on deep networks with more than 20 layers, single-hidden-layer wide networks with up to 16,384 hidden neurons, and even a simple Transformer architecture trained on both image classification (MNIST) and character-level language modeling (Tiny Shakespeare). This simple gradient-free method may offer a complementary perspective for understanding the self-organization and learning mechanisms of neural networks, and also provides an alternative route for building physically inspired deep learning systems.

</details>


### [71] [Predicting Male Fertility Using Machine Learning: A Semen Parameters Based Analysis with the VISEM Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08429)
*Shahnawaz Qureshi,Raja Khurram Shahzad,Muhammad Fozan,Emal Kawal,Syed Aziz Shah,Sattam Al-Anazi,Syed MuhammadZeeshan Iqbal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究利用VISEM数据集和LazyPredict框架，对40多种机器学习算法进行测试，发现Nearest Centroid分类器在基于精液参数（浓度、活力、形态）分类男性生育状态（可育、亚可育、不育）时达到94.2%的准确率，展示了机器学习在男性不育诊断中的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 男性不育是生殖健康中重要但常被低估的问题，传统精液分析作为临床评估基石存在主观性和局限性，因此需要更客观、准确的方法来分类男性生育状态以辅助临床决策。

Method: 使用VISEM数据集（85名参与者的精液样本），基于WHO标准将样本分为Fertile、Sub-Fertile、Infertile三类；经预处理和特征工程后，利用LazyPredict框架对40多种分类模型进行训练和评估，采用5折交叉验证和多分类ROC-AUC分析验证模型鲁棒性。

Result: Nearest Centroid分类器在40多种算法中表现最佳，准确率达到94.2%，优于支持向量机和二次判别分析等其他模型。

Conclusion: 机器学习模型能够快速、准确、客观地评估精液质量，有望支持男科和辅助生殖技术中的临床决策，并推动基于患者个体化治疗策略的制定。

Abstract: Male infertility is a significant yet often underdiagnosed aspect of reproductive health, with semen analysis serving as the cornerstone of clinical evaluation. To address this problem, this study investigates the use of machine learning algorithms to classify male fertility status based on key semen parameters, i.e., sperm concentration, motility, and morphology, using the VISEM dataset. This dataset includes semen samples from 85 participants, classified into three categories, i.e., Fertile, Sub-Fertile, and Infertile, according to the World Health Organization's criteria. After pre-processing and feature engineering, the dataset was used to train and assess multiple classification models using the LazyPredict framework. Among the more than 40 algorithms tested, the Nearest Centroid classifier achieved an accuracy of 94.2%, outperforming other models such as Support Vector Machines and Quadratic Discriminant Analysis. The model's robustness was validated using 5-fold cross-validation and multiclass ROC-AUC analysis. This study illustrates that machine learning models can provide fast, accurate, and objective assessments of semen quality, potentially supporting clinical decision-making in andrology and assisted reproductive technologies. These findings emphasize the growing potential of machine learning to enhance fertility diagnostics and inform patient-specific treatment strategies.

</details>


### [72] [MatBind: A Shared Embedding Space for Multimodal Materials Characterization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08470)
*Le Yang,Anoop K. Chandran,Jona Östreicher,Evgenii Sovetkin,Adrian Mirza,Sebastien Bompas,Bashir Kazimi,Pascal Friederich,Stefan Kesselheim,Kevin Maik Jablonka,Stefan Sandfeld*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了MatBind对比学习框架，将晶体结构、粉末X射线衍射、电子态密度和文本四种材料模态通过晶体结构作为物理锚点对齐到统一嵌入空间，实现了零样本跨模态检索和多模态组合查询性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 材料科学中多模态数据（原子结构、衍射图谱、态密度、自然语言）描述了同一物理对象的不同侧面，但现有方法将这些模态孤立存储和分析，难以跨表征边界进行关联或查询，因此需要一种统一的对齐框架。

Method: 采用对比学习框架，以晶体结构为中心物理锚点，将四种材料模态（晶体结构、模拟pXRD、DOS、文本）对齐到统一的嵌入空间，训练中无需显式配对所有模态对，通过共享表示实现零样本跨模态检索。

Result: 学习到的嵌入空间能够根据物理意义组织材料且无需显式监督；跨模态检索性能随查询时组合模态数量的增加而系统性提升；实现了从未显式配对的模态间的涌现式零样本检索。

Conclusion: 将异质材料数据视为同一物理实体的互补投影而非孤立数据源，不仅是一种实践选择，更符合底层物理规律。

Abstract: Fully characterizing a crystalline material requires integrating heterogeneous data sources -- atomic structures, diffraction patterns, electronic density of states, and natural language -- each of which captures a different facet of the same physical object. In practice, however, these modalities are stored and analyzed in isolation, making it difficult to relate or query materials across representational boundaries. We present MatBind, a contrastive learning framework that aligns four materials modalities -- crystal structure, powder X-ray diffraction (pXRD) simulated from structures, density of states (DOS), and text -- into a unified embedding space using crystal structure as the central physical anchor. The framework induces alignment between modalities never explicitly paired during training, enabling emergent zero-shot cross-modal retrieval as a direct consequence of the shared representation. The learned embedding space organizes materials according to physically meaningful properties without explicit supervision, and retrieval performance improves systematically when modalities are combined at query time. These results demonstrate that treating heterogeneous materials data as complementary projections of a single physical reality, rather than as isolated data sources, is not a practical choice but is consistent with the underlying physics.

</details>


### [73] [Frequency-Domain Multi-Modality Transportation Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08475)
*Jiewen Deng,Hangchen Liu,Junchen Li,Boyuan Zhang,Renhe Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FreMo是一种轻量级的频域多模态交通预测方法，通过模态度频率滤波器(MFF)和频率引导协同集成器(FSI)实现自适应、选择性的跨模态协同，在多个真实数据集上取得了最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法主要在时域操作或依赖粗粒度的特征融合，难以处理不同交通模态间不同的频谱特性以及频率维度上不均匀的交互，导致多模态交通预测精度受限。

Method: 提出FreMo框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) 模态度频率滤波器(MFF)，自适应地优化每个模态内的频谱成分，增强信息频率并抑制噪声；(2) 频率引导协同集成器(FSI)，根据各模态在每个频率上的相对贡献选择性聚合跨模态信息。FreMo采用解耦设计，将模态度频谱细化与跨模态协同分离，支持即插即用集成到通用时间序列主干网络中。

Result: 在真实数据集上的大量实验表明，FreMo在多种预测场景下持续超越最先进基线方法，表现出优异的性能和泛化能力。

Conclusion: 频域显式建模能够有效应对多模态交通预测中模态间频谱特性差异和频率维度不均匀交互的挑战，FreMo的轻量级设计兼具高效性与有效性，代码已开源。

Abstract: Multi-modality transportation refers to urban systems composed of multiple transportation modes, such as traffic flow and public transit, whose dynamics are coupled by shared temporal patterns. Accurate multi-modality transportation forecasting remains challenging because (1) different modalities exhibit distinct spectral characteristics and (2) interact unevenly across frequencies, whereas most existing methods operate primarily in the time domain or rely on coarse feature fusion. To address these limitations, we propose a lightweight yet effective Frequency-Domain Multi-Modality modeling (FreMo) that explicitly exploits the frequency domain to enable adaptive and selective cross-modality synergy. FreMo disentangles modality-wise spectral refinement from cross-modality synergy and supports plug-and-play integration with general time series backbones. Specifically, FreMo introduces a Modality-Wise Frequency Filter (MFF) to adaptively refine spectral components within each modality, emphasizing informative frequencies while suppressing noise. FreMo further incorporates a Frequency-Guided Synergy Integrator (FSI) that selectively aggregates information across modalities based on their relative contribution at each frequency, facilitating effective cross-modality knowledge sharing while mitigating negative transfer. Extensive experiments on real-world datasets show that FreMo consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, with superior performance and generalization across diverse forecasting scenarios. The code is available at https://github.com/beginner-sketch/FreMo.

</details>


### [74] [Ensemble Diversity Optimization for Subjective Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08493)
*Xia Cui,Ziyi Huang,N. R. Abeynayake*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出集成多样性优化（EDO）框架，通过可微分的统一目标联合优化集成权重、有效基数和校准，并在四个主观文本分类基准上显著改善了概率校准，同时保持了竞争性的F1分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主观性NLP任务中标注者之间系统性的不一致要求模型能够表示不确定性而非将其消解，现有方法在校准和不确定性建模方面仍存在不足。

Method: 提出EDO框架：利用Gumbel-Softmax松弛实现端到端集成学习，引入有符号多样性正则化器（在验证集上调参）以控制不确定性保留或抑制，集成软F1替代、类加权交叉熵和可靠性加权多样性等组件。

Result: 在ArMIS、ConvAbuse、HS-Brexit、MD-Agreement四个基准上，相比Soft-CE、Soft-MD、Top-5 Voting和WEL等基线，交叉熵降低40-78%，Brier分数降低，F1保持竞争力，与标注者分布对齐更好。

Conclusion: 联合优化集成结构与有符号多样性正则化器为监督学习中建模人类主观性提供了一种高效、模型无关的方法。

Abstract: Subjective NLP tasks often exhibit systematic annotator disagreement, requiring models that represent uncertainty rather than collapse it. We introduce Ensemble Diversity Optimization (EDO), a prediction-space framework that jointly optimizes ensemble weights, effective cardinality, and calibration through a unified differentiable objective. EDO learns ensemble composition and size end-to-end via Gumbel-Softmax relaxation and incorporates a signed diversity regularizer, tuned on validation data, to steer optimization toward either preserving or suppressing disagreement. This regularization prevents ensemble collapse and enables controlled navigation of the utility-calibration trade-off. The framework integrates a soft F1 surrogate, class-weighted cross-entropy to address imbalance, and reliability-weighted diversity to regulate intra-ensemble variability. Experiments on four subjective text-classification benchmarks (ArMIS, ConvAbuse, HS-Brexit, MD-Agreement) show that EDO substantially improves probabilistic calibration, reducing cross-entropy (40-78% depending on baseline) and lowering Brier scores relative to Soft-CE, Soft-MD, Top-5 Voting, and WEL, while maintaining competitive F1 and better alignment with annotator distributions. These results demonstrate that jointly optimizing ensemble structure with a signed diversity regularizer provides an efficient, model-agnostic approach for modeling human subjectivity in supervised learning.

</details>


### [75] [Systematic Evaluation of Learning Rate Scheduling Strategies Across Heterogeneous Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08511)
*Hafsa Mateen,Radu Timofte,Dmitry Ignatov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了学习率调度策略对30种神经网络架构在CIFAR-10上的影响，通过自动代码注入评估了25种调度器配置（共3,938个模型变体），发现调度器选择高度依赖架构，其中CosineAnnealingWarmRestarts和CyclicLR始终优于基础衰减策略，最佳配置达到86.45%的top-1准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习率调度策略对神经网络训练至关重要，但人工选择成本高且难以穷举。现有AutoML方法通常将调度器视为次级超参数，缺乏对其在不同架构下影响的系统性研究。

Method: 从LEMUR数据集中选取30种代表性架构（卷积和Transformer家族），通过自动源代码注入应用9个PyTorch调度器家族的25种配置，在CIFAR-10上评估了3,938个模型变体。

Result: 最佳配置top-1准确率86.45%，237个变体超过80%。CosineAnnealingWarmRestarts和CyclicLR一致优于基础衰减策略，调度器选择高度依赖架构。

Conclusion: 调度器选择与架构密切相关，不应视为次要超参数；研究结果已贡献至LEMUR nn-dataset，为基于原则的调度器选择提供了实用参考。

Abstract: Choosing a learning rate scheduling strategy is critical to neural network training, but manual selection is costly and rarely exhaustive. While classical AutoML approaches often treat the scheduler as a secondary hyperparameter, we systematically investigate its impact on classification accuracy across a diverse pool of architectures. We evaluated 30 representative architectures from convolutional and transformer families within the LEMUR neural network dataset. Through automated source-code injection, we applied 25 scheduler configurations across nine PyTorch families, evaluating a total of 3,938 model variants on CIFAR-10. Our best configuration achieved a top-1 accuracy of 86.45%, with 237 variants exceeding 80%. The results show that the choice of scheduler depends heavily on the architecture: CosineAnnealingWarmRestarts and CyclicLR consistently outperform basic decay strategies. The resulting accuracy landscape, contributed to the LEMUR nn-dataset, provides a practical reference for principled scheduler selection.

</details>


### [76] [Stop Guessing When to Stop Testing: Efficient Model Evaluation with Just Enough Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08522)
*Ofir Arviv,Kristjan Greenewald,Yotam Perlitz,Hadar Mulian,Michal Shmueli-Scheuer,Leshem Choshen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个自适应评估框架，通过结合序列检验和定制化停止准则，解决了固定规模基准测试在模型评估中效率与可靠性之间的权衡问题，在Open VLM Leaderboard上实现了高达80%的计算成本降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固定规模基准测试的刚性无法适应多样化的评估目标（模型排名、选择、开发测试），导致要么计算成本过高，要么可靠性不足，迫切需要一种自适应评估方法。

Method: 将序列检验（sequential testing）的统计范式与定制化停止准则相结合，包括递减回报检测和最小可检测效应量，构建自适应评估框架。

Result: 在Open VLM Leaderboard上，与固定规模评估相比，在保持统计显著性的前提下，实现了80%的计算成本降低（在2.5分置信区间宽度允许范围内）。

Conclusion: 序列检验为模型评估提供了原则性的效率-可靠性权衡方法，应被该领域广泛采用。

Abstract: The inherent rigidity of fixed-size benchmarks makes them an inefficient tool for model evaluation. Diverse evaluation objectives, including model ranking, model selection and testing throughout development, demand varying levels of statistical power. The mismatch between fixed sample sizes and these diverse needs results in either excessive computational cost or compromised reliability - a critical concern for model evaluation. To overcome these limitations, we call for adoption of sequential testing in our field. We provide an adaptive evaluation framework, that provides a principled way to navigate the trade-off between efficiency and reliability in model evaluation. Our framework combines the established statistical paradigm of sequential testing with stopping criteria tailored to common evaluation needs such as diminishing returns detection, and minimum detectable effect size. We demonstrate its ability to adaptively manage the efficiency-reliability trade-off on the Open VLM Leaderboard, including, for example, a 80% reduction in computational cost compared to fixed-size evaluation (with a 2.5-point CI width allowance) while maintaining statistical significance.

</details>


### [77] [CAAD: Causality-Aware Multivariate Time Series Anomaly Detection via Multi-Scale Alignment and Structural Causal Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08555)
*Xin Wang,Yunshi Wen,Yanan He,Haotian Xu,Youlan Zhao,Michel Ferreira Cardia Haddad,Tengfei Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CAAD框架，通过格兰杰因果一致性检验将异常检测重构为外生变量的连续验证，利用多尺度对齐和梯度矩阵监测因果关系的动态演化与拓扑结构偏差，在工业数据集上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有异常检测方法主要关注表征的时序相似性，忽视了系统故障和潜在异常中内部因果关系的破坏，导致检测精度不足。

Method: 将外生时序变量建模为残差，通过格兰杰因果一致性检验识别外部干预导致的显著偏差；采用多尺度对齐内化系统动力学，利用梯度矩阵监测内部因果关系的动态演化与关系拓扑的破坏。

Result: 在真实工业数据集上的大量实验表明，CAAD实现了高精度异常检测，性能优于大多数最先进的基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过量化动态演化与关系拓扑的因果偏差，CAAD能够捕获细微的因果偏移，实现精确的异常检测，弥补了现有方法忽视因果关系破坏的不足。

Abstract: The operational integrity of complex industrial systems relies on precise anomaly detection and diagnosis. The vast majority of existing methods narrowly focus on capturing temporal similarities of representations, often overlooking the disruption of internal causal relationships, which characterizes system failures and latent anomalies. In this paper, we propose a novel framework (CAAD) that reframes anomaly detection as the continuous verification of Granger causality consistency through exogenous variables.
  Specifically, the CAAD framework models exogenous time-series variables as residuals, identifying anomalies as significant deviations caused by external interventions. The proposed framework leverages multi-scale alignment to internalize system dynamics and utilizes a gradient-based matrix to monitor internal causal relationship breakdowns. By quantifying causal deviations of both dynamic evolution and relational topology, the CAAD is able to capture subtle causal shifts to achieve precise anomaly detection. Extensive experiments on real-world industrial datasets demonstrate that the CAAD achieves high-precision anomaly detection, outperforming most state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [78] [Contravariance Theory: Strong Alignment for Minimal Solutions to Hard Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08561)
*Dan Yamins,Aran Nayebi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明：对于足够困难任务的两个最小深度神经网络解，(i) 基于仿射映射的网络表示"弱对齐"能保证特权轴的"强对齐"；(ii) 对齐会沿网络层级"拉链式"向上传播，导致端到端任务优化中涌现特权轴。这形式化了Cao和Yamins [2024]的contravariance概念，并表明：任务足够强时，网络间比较的度量选择不敏感，且趋同演化几乎不可避免。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NeuroAI领域过去十五年的研究引发了关于如何比较深度神经网络与大脑、以及人工网络与真实脑网络间趋同演化程度的核心问题，亟需形式化理论框架来阐明这些关系。

Method: 通过数学证明，分析任意两个最小深度神经网络在解决足够困难任务时，网络表示的对齐性质及其层级传播机制，形式化定义弱对齐与强对齐的关系。

Result: 发现了对齐沿网络层级"拉链式"向上传播的机制，验证了任务难度足够时度量选择不敏感性和趋同演化的必然性。

Conclusion: 形式化了contravariance概念，为NeuroAI提供了理论基础：强任务驱动下，网络间比较的度量选择不敏感，且趋同演化是不可避免的。

Abstract: A series of results from the NeuroAI over the past fifteen years have raised core questions both about how to compare Deep Neural Network (DNN) models to the brain, and about how much convergent evolution to expect between artificial networks and real brain networks. Here, we show that for any two minimal DNN solutions to a sufficiently hard task: (i) "weak" alignment of network representations based on affine mappings guarantees "strong" alignment of privileged axes, and (ii) alignment "zippers" up the network hierarchy, causing the emergence of privileged axes from end-to-end task optimization. These results formalize the notion of contravariance from Cao and Yamins [2024], and illustrate important consequences for the theory of NeuroAI: with sufficiently strong tasks, choice of metric for inter-network comparison is not all that sensitive, and that convergent evolution is probably inevitable.

</details>


### [79] [Spectral Stability of Pseudoinverse-Based Extreme Learning Machine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08581)
*Bich Van Nguyen,Ngoc Anh Khong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从谱分解角度研究基于伪逆的极限学习机（ELM），揭示了隐藏层矩阵的最小奇异值及条件数对输出权重稳定性的影响，并实验比较了SVD方法在病态条件下优于迭代超幂法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ELM虽训练速度快，但其伪逆计算过程的数值稳定性高度依赖于隐藏层矩阵的条件数，现有研究对此缺乏系统的谱分析解释。

Method: 从谱分解视角分析ELM中伪逆运算的稳定性，定义最小奇异值与条件数作为量化指标，对比基于SVD的伪逆计算与迭代超幂法，并通过随机特征解释分析宽度依赖的条件性。

Result: SVD方法在病态条件下保持最可靠的稳定性，而迭代方法对谱性质更为敏感；隐藏层矩阵的奇异值结构从根本上决定了ELM的稳定性。

Conclusion: ELM的稳定性本质上由隐藏层矩阵的奇异值结构决定，SVD伪逆计算在病态条件下具有最优可靠性。

Abstract: Extreme Learning Machine (ELM) computes output weights analytically using the Moore-Penrose pseudoinverse. Although this leads to fast training, its numerical stability depends strongly on the conditioning of the hidden layer matrix. This paper studies pseudoinverse-based ELM from a spectral perspective. We show that the smallest singular value governs perturbation amplification in the output weights, while the condition number provides a quantitative measure of hidden-layer instability. We compare SVD-based pseudoinverse computation with iterative hyperpower methods and discuss width-dependent conditioning through a random feature interpretation. Experiments on synthetic matrices and ELM benchmarks show that SVD-based methods remain the most reliable under ill conditioning, while iterative methods are more sensitive to spectral properties. The results suggest that ELM stability is fundamentally governed by the singular value structure of the hidden layer matrix.

</details>


### [80] [Robust Bayesian Decision Making under Adversarial Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08590)
*Haripriya Harikumar,Sammie Katt,Yasir Zubayr Barlas,Samuel Kaski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种对抗鲁棒的决策感知实验设计方法，通过考虑最坏情况下的隐藏效应来增强决策的稳定性，实验表明该方法相比传统方法能产生更稳定可靠的决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有决策感知实验设计和主动学习方法通常假设模型设定正确，且隐含地依赖最优决策在真实扰动下的稳定性，但实际中实验结果常受隐藏或弱建模效应影响，导致决策最优性改变和误导性结论。

Method: 基于贝叶斯决策理论，形式化了对抗鲁棒的最优决策，推导了原则性的贝叶斯实验设计准则，该准则明确针对决策稳定性而非名义最优性。

Result: 在合成和真实科学数据集上的实验表明，传统决策感知设计可能快速收敛到高置信度但脆弱的决策，而所提出的鲁棒感知方法在对抗变化下能产生更稳定可靠的决策。

Conclusion: 考虑对抗鲁棒性的实验设计能够有效提升决策在实际扰动中的稳定性，为科学实验中的可靠决策提供了新的方法论支持。

Abstract: Scientific experiments are often designed to maximize information gain, yet in many applications the primary objective is to support reliable downstream decision-making. Existing decision-aware experimental design and active learning methods typically assume well-specified outcome models and implicitly rely on the stability of the optimal decision under real-world perturbations. In practice, however, experimental outcomes are frequently influenced by hidden or weakly modeled effects, which can substantially alter decision optimality and lead to misleading conclusions. We study sequential adversarially robust decision-aware experimental design, where data acquisition has to take into account information gain against plausible worst-case unexpected effects, modeled here as variation in adversarial variables. Building on Bayesian decision theory, we formalize an adversarially robust optimal decision under this setting and derive a principled Bayesian experimental design criterion. The criterion explicitly targets decision stability rather than nominal optimality. Experiments on synthetic and real-world scientific datasets show that conventional decision-aware design can converge rapidly to high confidence yet fragile decisions, while our robustness-aware approach yields decisions that are significantly more stable and reliable under adversarial variation.

</details>


### [81] [Federated Deep Learning for Privacy-Preserving Cardiovascular Disease Risk Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08595)
*Hyunho Mo,Djura Smits,Mahlet A. Birhanu,Maarten J. G. Leening,Daniel Bos,Pim van der Harst,Esther E. Bron*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种联邦深度学习方法，用于跨两个异质性人群队列（Lifelines, N=148,230；Rotterdam Study, N=10,155）进行隐私保护的CVD风险预测。联邦学习训练的深度生存模型在C统计量上优于各中心的本地模型，证明了在不共享患者级数据的情况下，利用异质性队列提升预测性能的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统CVD风险预测模型多依赖单中心或集中式数据，扩展至多中心时受限于隐私法规和数据共享约束；同时，不同机构的数据在规模、人群特征和结局定义上存在异质性，使得联邦学习的应用面临挑战。

Method: 采用联邦深度生存分析方法，在两个异质性队列（Lifelines：148,230名参与者、自我报告结局；Rotterdam Study：10,155名参与者、临床链接结局）上协作训练模型，无需传输原始患者数据。

Result: 联邦学习训练的模型在Rotterdam Study上C-statistic从0.728提升至0.739（95% CI: 0.728-0.749），在Lifelines上从0.783提升至0.787（95% CI: 0.780-0.792），均优于本地训练模型。

Conclusion: 联邦深度学习能够利用异质性队列提升CVD风险预测性能，同时保护患者级数据隐私，为跨机构协作建模提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Cardiovascular disease risk prediction models often rely on data from a single institution or centrally pooled datasets. Extending these models across institutions could be limited by privacy regulations and constraints on sharing patient-level data. Federated learning enables collaborative model development without transferring sensitive patient data, but its application in healthcare remains challenging because datasets often differ in size, population characteristics, and outcome definitions. In this study, we present a federated deep learning approach for privacy-preserving cardiovascular disease risk prediction that integrates two population-based cohorts with different characteristics: Lifelines, including 148,230 participants meeting the study inclusion criteria with self-reported outcomes, and the Rotterdam Study, including a smaller cohort of 10,155 participants with digitally linked clinical outcomes. Model performance was primarily evaluated on the Rotterdam Study because of its complete follow-up. Deep survival models trained using federated learning achieved higher predictive performance than models trained locally without federation. For the Rotterdam Study, the C-statistic increased from 0.728 (95% CI: 0.717-0.739) to 0.739 (95% CI: 0.728-0.749). For Lifelines, the C-statistic increased from 0.783 (95% CI: 0.775-0.791) to 0.787 (95% CI: 0.780-0.792). These findings suggest that federated deep learning across heterogeneous cohorts can improve cardiovascular disease risk prediction while preserving the privacy of individual-level patient data.

</details>


### [82] [Steering Neural Network Training through Interpretable Constraints Based on Partial Dependence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08641)
*Yann Claes,Pierre Geurts,Vân Anh Huynh-Thu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于偏依赖的神经网络引导方法，使模型的平均响应符合特定功能领域知识，并在回归问题（包括动力系统预测）上验证其比未约束模型表现更好、数据效率更高，且解释结果与用户知识一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前可解释机器学习研究多集中于开发解释技术，但缺乏对解释质量的评估，更少研究如何调整模型以产生符合先验知识的解释（即解释引导学习）。现有方法主要针对分类问题，且假设已知重要输入特征或区域，缺乏适用于回归问题的通用方法。

Method: 提出一种基于偏依赖（partial dependence）的神经网络引导方法，通过约束模型对特定特征的平均响应，使其符合用户提供的功能领域知识（functional domain knowledge），从而在训练过程中引导模型学习。

Result: 在多个回归问题（包括动力系统预测）上，该方法训练的模型比未约束模型表现更好，数据效率更高；同时，前者获得的解释与用户提供的先验知识一致，而后者则不一致。

Conclusion: 基于偏依赖的解释引导学习能有效将领域知识融入神经网络训练，提升模型性能和可解释性，尤其适用于回归问题。

Abstract: Over the last few years, there has been an increased interest in making machine learning models more interpretable. Although a great deal of effort goes into developing techniques for interpreting the interactions learned by a given model, fewer studies focus on assessing the quality of such explanations. Even fewer focus on how to adjust the model to produce explanations faithful to prior knowledge, a process known as explanation-guided learning. Furthermore, most approaches in this area focus on classification problems and usually assume prior knowledge about which input features or regions are most important. In this work, we introduce a new approach to steering neural networks based on partial dependence, such that their average response to certain features aligns with specific functional domain knowledge about the problem. We empirically demonstrate on a range of regression problems, including dynamical systems forecasting, that models whose training has been controlled using our method perform better than unconstrained models and are more data-efficient. Moreover, we highlight that interpretations obtained from the former actually align with the user-provided knowledge, whereas those obtained from the latter do not.

</details>


### [83] [BiSCo-LLM: Lookup-Free Binary Spherical Coding for Extreme Low-Bit Large Language Model Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08643)
*Yuantian Shao,Peisong Wang,Zhilei Liu,Chuangyi Li,Yuanteng Chen,Pengcheng Xie,Yiwu Yao,Zhihui Wei,Jian Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BiSCo-LLM提出一种无码本的二进制球面编码框架，用于大语言模型的极端低位权重复，通过球面二值化、残差编码和分类恢复蒸馏，在2比特权重下达到优秀性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有低位压缩方法中，标量/组量化在接近2比特/权重时表示能力有限，而向量量化虽有更强表示能力但需引入显式码本和索引查找，带来额外存储开销。为此需要一种无需码本、表示能力强的极低位压缩方案。

Method: 三部分核心流水线：(1) 将局部权重块映射到单位超球面并二值化为球面码，主载荷为比特打包的符号流而非显式VQ质心；(2) 残差BSQ阶段编码基球面编解码器遗留的重建误差，提供无码本的率失真路径；(3) 逐Transformer模块类别替换后进行类别级恢复蒸馏，降低局部权重重建与组装模型行为之间的不匹配。另设8比特保护通道作为敏感通道的辅助稳定机制。

Result: 存储预算包含二进制码、神经解码器、保护通道载荷、LoRA适配器和元数据。在极低位压缩场景下实现了优于现有方法的性能。

Conclusion: BiSCo-LLM通过无码本二进制球面编码框架有效解决了极低位权重复的表示能力与存储效率矛盾，为LLM部署中的内存和带宽瓶颈提供了新方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly constrained by memory capacity, weight bandwidth, and checkpoint storage during deployment. Existing low-bit compression methods mainly follow two directions. Scalar or group-wise quantization is simple and compatible with efficient low-precision kernels, but its representation capacity becomes limited when the target budget approaches 2 bits per weight. Vector-quantized weight compression provides a richer block-level representation, but usually introduces explicit codebooks, index lookup, and additional storage accounting. This paper presents BiSCo-LLM, a codebook-free binary spherical coding framework for extreme low-bit LLM weight compression. The core pipeline is built on three components. First, local weight chunks are mapped onto a unit hypersphere and binarized into compact spherical codes, so that the main payload is a bit-packed sign stream rather than explicit VQ centroids. Second, a residual BSQ stage encodes the reconstruction error left by the base spherical codec, providing an explicit rate-distortion path without stored codebooks. Third, category-wise recovery distillation is performed after replacing each Transformer module category, reducing the mismatch between local weight reconstruction and assembled model behavior. A small 8-bit protected-channel path is used as an auxiliary stabilization mechanism for sensitive channels and is counted separately from the BSQ payload. The reported storage budget includes binary codes, neural decoders, protected-channel payloads, LoRA adapters, and metadata.

</details>


### [84] [Multi-Modal, Multi-Environment Machine Teaching for Robust Reward Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08647)
*Ali Larian,Qian Lin,Chang Zong Wu,Daniel S. Brown*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文分析了不同反馈模态对逆强化学习中奖励函数约束的强度差异，并提出了一种跨多环境的分层机器教学算法，通过贪婪选择信息性环境并策略性查询低成本反馈，学习对环境动态鲁棒的奖励函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有逆强化学习的教学分析局限于单环境、仅演示的场景，未探究异构反馈模态和环境动态如何共同约束跨环境泛化的奖励函数。演示在单个MDP中会纠缠奖励信息与环境特定结构，导致部署到新环境时泛化失败。

Method: 首先理论分析不同反馈模态对奖励的约束能力，证明在无限数据下比较约束（comparisons）比演示约束（demonstrations）施加更强的全局约束。然后提出分层机器教学算法：上层贪婪选择能暴露互补奖励约束的信息性环境，下层在选定环境中策略性地查询低成本反馈。

Result: 在相同反馈预算下，所提方法相比均匀教学基线实现了显著更低的遗憾值和更强的向未见环境泛化的能力。

Conclusion: 跨环境、多模态的教学对于学习对环境动态鲁棒的奖励函数至关重要，比较反馈在多环境设置中比演示具有更强的约束优势。

Abstract: As autonomous agents are increasingly deployed across diverse operational contexts, aligning their behavior with human intent demands reward functions that remain robust to such changes rather than overfitting to any single environment. Inverse reinforcement learning (IRL) provides a principled way to infer such objectives from human feedback. However, existing analyses of optimal teaching approaches for IRL focus on single-environment, demonstration-only settings, leaving underexplored how heterogeneous feedback modalities and environment dynamics jointly constrain reward functions that generalize across multiple environments. Because demonstrations in one MDP entangle reward information with that environments specific structure, the resulting rewards frequently fail to generalize when the agent is deployed in a new setting. We first analyze how different feedback modalities constrain rewards, showing that, in the unlimited-data regime, comparisons impose strictly stronger global constraints than other modalities. Beyond this theoretical analysis, we introduce a hierarchical machine teaching algorithm for reward learning that operates across multiple MDPs. The algorithm first greedily selects informative environments that expose complementary reward constraints, then strategically queries low-cost feedback within those environments. Empirically, our method achieves substantially lower regret and stronger generalization to held-out environments than uniform teaching baselines under identical feedback budgets, demonstrating the importance of multi-environment, multi-modal teaching for learning dynamics-robust reward functions.

</details>


### [85] [A Practical Investigation of Training-free Relaxed Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08690)
*Guoxuan Xia,Luka Ribar,Paul Balanca*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对无训练松弛推测解码技术进行了系统研究，统一了现有方法框架，并通过实验对比发现：松弛方法需要仔细评估能力损失，且许多方法依赖强大的草稿模型，不适合轻量级专用草稿器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准推测解码虽然无损失，但严格保证采样分布不变可能限制了加速潜力。近期工作表明放松这一保证可以进一步提升速度，实现能力-速度的权衡甚至能力提升，因此有必要系统研究松弛式推测解码方法。

Method: 无训练（training-free）的松弛推测解码技术；将现有方法统一到共享框架中；在当代设置下进行基准测试。

Result: 松弛方法需要大量的能力评估；许多松弛方法依赖草稿模型本身是良好语言模型，不适合轻量级专用多token预测草稿器。

Conclusion: 松弛解码虽能提速，但需谨慎评估能力损失，且其适用范围受限于草稿模型的类型和质量。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates sampling from an autoregressive LLM by using a faster auxiliary model to draft tokens which are then verified in parallel by the LLM. Standard speculative decoding is lossless: its rejection and resampling steps exactly preserve the LLM's sampling distribution. Recent work argues that relaxing this strict guarantee can yield further speed-ups, controlled capability-speed trade-offs, or even capability gains. We practically investigate training-free relaxed speculative decoding techniques, unify existing approaches within a shared framework, benchmark them on contemporary settings, and distil takeaways and empirical findings for practitioners. Important takeaways include: relaxation can require considerable capability evaluation unlike lossless speculative decoding, and many relaxed approaches rely on a drafter that is a good language model, making them unsuited for lightweight dedicated multi-token-prediction drafters.

</details>


### [86] [EdgeRefine: Privacy-Utility Balance for Graphs via Jaccard Sampling under Edge Differential Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08659)
*Wenxiu Ding,Muzhi Liu,Zheng Yan,Mingjun Wang,Yifan Zhao,Qiao Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出EdgeRefine，一种通过自适应边细化来改善图神经网络边级差分隐私中隐私-效用权衡的局部差分隐私框架。它利用Jaccard相似度估计边存在概率，根据隐私预算ε确定真假边比例并分别采样，用独立采样率k控制总边数。实验表明，EdgeRefine在隐私预算ε=2.5时，节点分类精度比最先进基线提升17.8%-19.7%，图分类精度退化仅约5%，且对图重构攻击具有强鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GNN差分隐私方法通过向邻接矩阵所有元素注入噪声来保护边级隐私，但强隐私需要更多噪声，导致效用严重下降，隐私-效用权衡成为实际隐私保护图学习的主要障碍。

Method: EdgeRefine框架包含三个步骤：(1) 使用Jaccard相似度估计每条边的存在概率；(2) 根据概率对边排序，利用隐私预算ε确定真是边与虚假边的比例；(3) 基于概率排名分别采样真实边和虚假边，并用独立采样率k控制总边数，生成稀疏可靠的私有化图。

Result: 在ε=2.5时，ACM数据集上GAT节点分类提升17.8%，Cora数据集上GCN节点分类提升19.7%。图分类精度退化仅约5%。图重构攻击下，相对绝对误差在所有隐私预算下均高于1，Cora上平均1.962，AMAP上平均1.472。

Conclusion: EdgeRefine通过自适应边细化有效改善了差分隐私图学习的隐私-效用权衡，在强隐私保护下仍能保持与无噪声基线相当的精度，并显著优于现有隐私保护方法，同时具备强隐私泄露抵抗能力。

Abstract: Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) have shown considerable success in learning from graph-structured data, but their use in privacy-sensitive areas remains difficult because graph structure can leak sensitive link information. To satisfy edge-level differential privacy, a common approach is to inject noise into all elements of the graph's adjacency matrix, thereby obfuscating the existence of any single edge. However, stronger privacy requires more noise, and excessive noise reduces utility, making the privacy-utility balance a major barrier to practical privacy-preserving graph learning.
  To address this issue, we propose EdgeRefine, a local differential privacy framework that improves this trade-off through adaptive edge refinement. EdgeRefine first estimates edge-existence probabilities using Jaccard similarity and ranks edges for noisy edge removal. To ensure the sparsity and reliability of the final graph, it uses the privacy budget $ε$ to determine the ratio of true to false edges, samples them separately based on this probability ranking, and controls the total number of edges with a separate sampling rate $k$. Extensive experiments show that EdgeRefine achieves accuracy comparable to the noise-free baseline and substantially outperforms other privacy-preserving methods across datasets and GNN architectures. Under privacy budget $ε= 2.5$, EdgeRefine improves node classification accuracy over state-of-the-art baselines by 17.8\% on ACM under GAT and 19.7\% on Cora under GCN. In graph classification, it achieves an average accuracy degradation of around 5\% compared to the noise-free baseline. Under graph reconstruction attacks, EdgeRefine maintains relative absolute error levels above 1 across all privacy budgets, averaging 1.962 on Cora and 1.472 on AMAP, indicating strong resilience against privacy leakage.

</details>


### [87] [Dimensionality Reduction Meets Network Science: Sensemaking on UMAP's kNN Graph](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08746)
*Duen Horng Chau,Donghao Ren,Fred Hohman,Dominik Moritz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: UMAP内部构建的kNN图蕴含丰富的高维流形信息，通过应用PageRank、k-core分解和聚类系数等标准图算法，可有效增强数据理解，在代表性点选取、密度聚类等任务上表现优于或媲美专用方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 典型UMAP工作流仅关注低维嵌入，忽略了其内部构建的kNN图所编码的高维流形信息，这些信息在降维到2D时会发生失真。

Method: 对UMAP构建的kNN图应用三种标准图算法：PageRank识别代表性数据点、k-core分解发现密集核心与稀疏外围区域、聚类系数检测高度相似的紧密邻域。在MNIST和Fashion MNIST上进行定量与定性评估。

Result: 基于图的这些分析方法不仅实用，而且与专用方法（如k-medoids、HDBSCAN）相比具有竞争力或互补性。

Conclusion: UMAP内部的kNN图具有未被充分利用的潜力，利用标准图算法可以增强数据理解，为高维数据探索提供新的视角。

Abstract: While UMAP is widely used for exploring high-dimensional data, typical workflows focus on its lower-dimensional embedding, largely overlooking the rich k-nearest-neighbor (kNN) graph that UMAP constructs internally. This graph encodes the data manifold in its original high-dimensional space, before the distortion that UMAP's 2D projection introduces. We demonstrate the untapped potential of this internal representation, showing how standard graph algorithms applied to this graph enhance data sensemaking: (1) PageRank identifies representative data points, (2) k-core decomposition reveals dense core regions versus sparse periphery, and (3) clustering coefficient detects tight-knit neighborhoods with highly-similar data points. Through quantitative and qualitative evaluation on MNIST and Fashion MNIST, we show that these graph-based analyses are not only practical but also competitive with or complementary to purpose-built methods (e.g., k-medoids for exemplar selection, HDBSCAN for density-based clustering).

</details>


### [88] [Resample or Reroute? Budget-Aware Test-Time Model Selection for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08665)
*Teng-Ruei Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出预算感知的测试时模型选择策略（Resample-or-Route, RoR），在给定每查询预算和不完美验证器下，最优分配重采样与重路由预算单元以最大化期望正确性，实验表明该方法在成本-质量Pareto前沿上优于多种基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM路由工作通常假设单次提交即可达到最优，但理论上重采样可以恢复单次路由无法捕获的选择空间；然而实际部署中缺乏带正确标签的完美预言机且预算有限。此前未有工作将重采样已提交模型与重路由到替代模型视为对同一预算的竞争性使用。

Method: 提出Resample-or-Route（RoR）在线分配策略，基于每单位成本的边际正确性估计，在重采样与重路由之间动态分配预算。在11个开源模型、4个难度不同的基准上重构多轮正确性张量进行回放实验。

Result: RoR在成本-质量Pareto前沿上优于单路由、单提交路由、预算感知Best-of-K、级联及随机分配等基线；在异构性最强的基准上增益最大；消融实验表明增益依赖于验证器质量，质量下降时增益缩小；在提供商价格向量和无标签一致性验证器下结论具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 预算约束下，将重采样与重路由在同一框架内联合优化能够更有效地提升LLM服务的成本效益；验证器质量是关键瓶颈。

Abstract: Routing among large language models (LLMs) trades response quality against serving cost, motivated by the reported gap between deployed routers and a per-instance oracle. Recent analysis shows that test-time resampling can recover per-instance selection headroom that no single-commit router captures; however, that guarantee holds only under an idealized oracle equipped with correctness labels and an unconstrained budget, neither of which a deployed system has. To the best of our knowledge, no previous work treats resampling the committed model and rerouting to an alternative model as competing uses of a single per-query cost budget. Therefore, this work formulates budget-aware test-time model selection: given a per-query budget and an imperfect verifier, allocate each unit of budget between resampling and rerouting so that expected correctness is maximized. An online resample-or-reroute (RoR) allocation policy driven by estimated marginal correctness per unit cost is proposed, and its behavior is grounded in the recoverability asymmetry between selection and sampling. Replay experiments on newly regenerated multi-draw correctness tensors from an eleven-model open-weight pool over four benchmarks of differing difficulty show that the proposed RoR policy attains a favorable cost-quality Pareto front relative to single-route, one-commit-router, budget-aware best-of-K, cascade, and random-allocation baselines for the tested pools, with the largest gains on the most heterogeneous benchmark; an ablation further shows the gains are verifier-gated, shrinking as verifier quality degrades, and robustness replays under a provider price vector and a label-free agreement verifier delineate where the conclusions carry over.

</details>


### [89] [SLORR: Simple and Efficient In-Training Low-Rank Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08754)
*David González-Martínez,Shiwei Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SLORR是一种简单、无状态、保持架构的原位低秩正则化框架，基于Hoyer稀疏度量和核范数，通过GPU友好的近似实现，在ImageNet-1K和LLM预训练中有效提升模型可压缩性，且训练开销极低（<8%和<1%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有训练时低秩正则化方法存在依赖SVD分解、修改模型架构引入额外参数、或依赖有状态缓存等局限性，需一种简单、无状态、保持架构的正则化框架。

Method: 提出SLORR框架，包含基于Hoyer稀疏度量和核范数的两种变体，直接对原始权重矩阵进行正则化，使用GPU友好的近似计算前向和反向传播，并提供近似保证。

Result: 在ImageNet-1K上（ResNet-50、ViT-B/16、ViT-L/16继续训练及ResNet-18预训练）引入<8%训练开销诱导可压缩性；在135M和560M规模的LLM预训练中，SLORR训练的压缩模型性能损失显著低于未正则化模型，平均训练开销<1%。

Conclusion: SLORR是一种高效、轻量级的低秩正则化方法，能在几乎不增加训练开销的情况下提升模型的可压缩性，适用于图像分类和大语言模型。

Abstract: Low-rank factorization is widely used to compress neural networks, but modern models are often not naturally amenable to aggressive factorization without significant accuracy loss. Existing training-time low-rank regularizers can improve compressibility, but they often require SVDs of large weight matrices, modify the model architecture (introducing additional trainable parameters), or rely on stateful cached quantities. To address these limitations, we introduce SLORR, a simple, stateless, and architecture-preserving framework for in-training low-rank regularization, instantiated with two main variants based on the Hoyer sparsity metric and the nuclear norm. SLORR directly regularizes the original weight matrices using GPU-friendly approximations for the forward and backward passes of the regularizers, for which we provide approximation guarantees. We first evaluate SLORR on ImageNet-1K across short-horizon continued training of ResNet-50, ViT-B/16, and ViT-L/16, and pretraining of ResNet-18, where SLORR induces compressibility while introducing less than 8% training overhead. We further evaluate SLORR-Hoyer in LLM pretraining at 135M and 560M scales: SLORR-trained compressed models preserve performance substantially better than unregularized models while adding less than 1% average training overhead.

</details>


### [90] [MPFlow: Learning Budgeted Max-Flow Optimization on the Lightning Network with Deep Graph Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08703)
*Harrison Rush,Vincent Davis,Simone Antonelli,Vikash Singh,Jesse Shrader,Emanuele Rossi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究比特币闪电网络中流动性放置问题：给定固定预算，如何选择通道以最大化路由容量。提出了基于图强化学习的方法，将问题建模为预算约束下的组合优化，通过消息传递策略网络和PPO算法求解，并在真实闪电网络快照上验证了有效性，已在生产环境中部署并执行了4640次通道开立决策，累计分配267.3 BTC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决比特币闪电网络中节点的流动性放置问题：在固定预算约束下，如何选择开通哪些通道以最大化路由容量，这是LN网络中提升支付成功率和效率的关键问题。

Method: 将问题建模为预算约束下的组合优化问题，即选择k条边添加以最大化s-t最大流（路由容量的理论衡量指标）；采用图强化学习方法，结合消息传递策略网络与PPO算法及动作掩码；训练中引入枢纽排除课程（hub-exclusion curriculum），移除训练子图中的顶级枢纽节点，使策略学习容量感知的放置而非简单的枢纽连接。

Result: 在真实闪电网络快照上的大量实验中，该方法在最大流目标上始终优于强启发式基线，且在不同种子和未见过的图上表现一致。已在生产环境中部署执行了4640次通道开立决策，累计分配267.3 BTC（超过1600万美元），横跨30个托管节点。

Conclusion: 提出的图强化学习方法能够有效解决闪电网络中的流动性放置问题，在理论指标和实际部署中均表现出色，具备实际应用价值。

Abstract: We address liquidity placement in the Bitcoin Lightning Network (LN): given a fixed budget, which channels should a node open to maximize its routing capacity? We cast this as a budget-constrained combinatorial optimization problem on graphs, selecting $k$ edge additions that maximize $s$--$t$ max-flow, a theory-grounded measure of routing capacity, and solve it with graph reinforcement learning. Our lightweight agent combines a message-passing policy network with proximal policy optimization (PPO) and action masking, and is trained under a hub-exclusion curriculum: the network's top hubs are removed from training subgraphs, forcing the policy to learn capacity-aware placement rather than hub attachment. In extensive experiments on real Lightning Network snapshots, our method consistently outperforms strong heuristic baselines on the max-flow objective across multiple seeds and unseen graphs. The agent has been deployed in production for peer recommendations, executing 4640 channel-open decisions that cumulatively allocate 267.3 BTC over $16 million across 30 managed nodes.

</details>


### [91] [Deep Learning for Joint Narrowband Interference Cancellation and Soft Demodulation in OFDM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08717)
*Emmanouil Kavvousanos,Francky Catthoor,Vassilis Paliouras*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一个统一的深度学习框架NBI-CNet+LLR-CNet，用于OFDM系统中窄带干扰消除和鲁棒软解调，消除了传统方法的错误平层，在SIR=-10dB时仅与最优迭代基线相差0.2-0.5dB，且无需重训练即可泛化到任意FFT尺寸。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统压缩感知NBI抑制方法存在高顺序延迟和结构化非高斯残差，导致LLR不可靠、解码器饱和及严重错误平层，需要解决这一流水线不匹配问题。

Method: 提出联合深度学习框架：(1) NBI-CNet：物理信息驱动的卷积架构，单次前向传播估计NBI参数并消除多音干扰，无需先验干扰计数；(2) LLR-CNet：结构白化器，将非高斯后抑制残差映射为校准的软度量。

Result: 相比EOMP-IDS算法计算复杂度降低60%(N=2048, Q=64)；在SIR=-10dB严重干扰下，BLER=10^-4时与最优迭代基线仅差0.2-0.5dB；在SIR=10dB、Q=12时编码增益超过3dB；消除了2×10^-4的错误平层；尺度不变设计无需重训练泛化到任意FFT尺寸。

Conclusion: 该统一深度学习框架有效解决了NBI抑制与软解调之间的流水线不匹配问题，在复杂度、性能和泛化能力上均优于传统方法。

Abstract: Narrowband interference (NBI) severely degrades orthogonal frequency-division multiplexing (OFDM) systems by corrupting subcarriers and rendering classical soft demodulation ineffective. Conventional compressed-sensing (CS) mitigation exhibits high sequential latency and leaves structured, non-Gaussian residuals that cause log-likelihood ratio (LLR) unreliability, decoder saturation, and severe error floors when employing classical Gaussian demappers. We resolve this pipeline mismatch using a unified deep learning framework for joint NBI cancellation and robust soft demodulation. First, NBI-CNet employs a physics-informed convolutional architecture to estimate NBI parameters and remove multi-tone interference in a single forward pass. Without requiring prior knowledge of the active interferer count, NBI-CNet reduces computational complexity by up to 60% ($N{=}2048, Q{=}64$) compared to the state-of-the-art EOMP-IDS algorithm. Second, LLR-CNet acts as a structural whitener by mapping non-Gaussian post-mitigation residuals onto well-calibrated soft metrics. Simulations demonstrate that this joint framework eliminates the error floors inherent to traditional baselines across dense grids. Under severe interference ($\text{SIR}{=}{-}10$ dB), the pipeline operates within a $0.2$ to $0.5$ dB SNR margin of the optimal iterative baseline at a target block error rate (BLER) of $10^{-4}$. Under mild interference ($\text{SIR}{=}10$ dB) with heavy spectral overlap ($Q{=}12$), where classical greedy algorithms erroneously subtract valid data components and corrupt the payload, NBI-CNet avoids signal-peak confusion to deliver a coding gain exceeding $3$ dB. Finally, the architecture circumvents the $2{\times}10^{-4}$ error floor triggered by interferer-estimation errors, while its scale-invariant design enables robust generalization across arbitrary FFT sizes without retraining.

</details>


### [92] [Latent Memory Palace: Reasoning for Control as Autoregressive Variational Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08724)
*Chuning Zhu,Eva Xu,Jose Barreiros,Krishnan Srinivasan,Paarth Shah,Abhishek Gupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Latent Memory Palace (LMP)方法，将推理视为具有自回归潜变量分布的变分推断，通过在潜空间中进行迭代自适应检索（类似记忆宫殿）来实现控制策略的推理，在仿真和真实世界任务中取得优异表现，并展现出可解释的自适应测试时计算分配能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型已展现自适应推理能力，但将其迁移到连续控制策略上仍具挑战性，因为直接在语言空间进行推理缺乏空间理解和精细运动所需的粒度。

Method: 将推理形式化为具有自回归潜变量分布的变分推断；推导出一种潜空间强化学习技术来可微地优化其变分下界；同时提出变长动作分词器LMP-tok。

Result: LMP-π在仿真和真实世界任务中取得强实证表现；展现出可解释的自适应测试时计算分配；LMP-tok显著提升下游自回归策略的性能。

Conclusion: 通过变分推断的视角为控制策略的潜空间推理提供了新的理论框架，潜空间中自回归信息组织可以实现类似语言模型的自适应推理能力。

Abstract: Human decision-making is highly flexible -- some actions are taken immediately; others require longer deliberation. Language models have exhibited a similar capacity for adaptive "reasoning." However, transferring this capability to continuous control policies has been challenging, as directly reasoning in language space may lack the granularity for spatial understanding and precise motions. In this work, we show that reasoning for control policies can emerge by organizing information in an autoregressive latent space reminiscent of a memory palace, where retrieval is iterative and adaptive. Our method, Latent Memory Palace (LMP), formulates reasoning as variational inference with an autoregressive latent distribution. We derive a latent-space reinforcement learning technique to tractably optimize its variational lower bound. The resulting policy, LMP-$π$, achieves strong empirical performance in simulation and real-world domains while exhibiting interpretable, adaptive allocation of test-time compute. We further show that the same framework yields a variable-length action tokenizer, LMP-$\texttt{tok}$, which significantly improves the performance of downstream autoregressive policies. Together, these results present a new perspective on latent reasoning for control through the lens of variational inference.

</details>


### [93] [Super Weights in LLMs and the Failure of Selective Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08733)
*Shreyas Subramanian,Adewale Akinfaderin,Akarsha Sehwag*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现"超级权重"（Super Weights）在LLM中并非普遍存在，且孤立训练这些参数会导致模型性能崩溃（降至随机水平），而使用LoRA等结构化低秩更新方法即使只更新极少量参数也能成功。研究表明参数重要性不等于参数可训练性，有效微调依赖于对整层的结构化分解，而非针对个别重要参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证"超级权重"（Super Weights）是否普遍存在于所有LLM中，以及针对这些参数进行训练是否能带来性能提升，从而探究参数重要性与可训练性之间的关系。

Method: 在OLMo-1B和OLMo-7B模型上，分别进行：1) 孤立训练Super Weights（100到8,192个参数）；2) 扩展到局部邻域（最多36K参数）；3) 在相同down_proj层中训练随机位置；4) 使用Vanilla LoRA（仅0.16%参数）；5) 将LoRA更新限制在Super Weight坐标位置。所有实验进行10-seed消融统计。

Result: 孤立训练Super Weights导致准确率降至随机猜测水平；扩展到36K邻域仍无改善；训练相同层中随机位置反而提升性能；Vanilla LoRA仅用0.16%参数取得成功；将LoRA限制在Super Weight坐标位置效果与基线无统计差异。

Conclusion: 参数重要性并不等同于参数在孤立情况下的可训练性；有效微调依赖于对整个层进行结构化分解（如低秩结构），而非针对个别重要权重进行训练。

Abstract: Recent work identified Super Weights, individual parameters whose removal degrades model performance by orders of magnitude. We show that this degradation due to pruning Super Weights does not universally apply to all LLMs. Furthermore, if these parameters are so important, Super Weight-aware training should be effective. We show the opposite. Training Super Weights in isolation (100 to 8,192 parameters) drops accuracy to random-guessing levels on both OLMo-1B and OLMo-7B, and expanding to local neighborhoods of up to 36K parameters provides no improvement. The failure is specific to Super Weight coordinates: training an equal number of randomly chosen positions in the same down_proj layers instead improves over the baseline, so the collapse comes from targeting Super Weights, not from sparsity itself. Vanilla LoRA, updating every position in attention weight matrices through low-rank structure, succeeds with only 0.16% of parameters, and applying the same low-rank update to down_proj succeeds as well. A 10-seed ablation confirms that constraining LoRA updates at positions corresponding to Super Weight coordinates yields statistically indistinguishable results. These findings establish that parameter importance does not imply parameter trainability in isolation, and that effective fine-tuning relies on structured decompositions over entire layers rather than targeting individually important weights.

</details>

### [94] [CTA-Pipelining: A Latency-Oriented Spatial Scaling Method for Multi-GPU Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07862)
*Tingkai Liu,Muralidhar Andoorveedu,Sanjoy Das,Sanjay Patel,Volodymyr Kindratenko*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 针对大规模语言模型服务中的延迟约束问题，本文提出CTA-pipelining执行范式，利用共享内存多GPU系统在Cooperative Thread Array级别建立依赖关系，实现跨GPU的依赖内核并发执行。在8-GPU H200和B200系统上的实验表明，相比微批处理和Tensor Parallelism，该方法在2层GEMM（MLP操作）上分别降低延迟达31.8%和29.6%，并可正交结合TP进一步优化延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多GPU系统已发展为紧密集成的共享内存结构，但软件仍将其视为高速网络；同时LLM服务对延迟敏感，使得GPU工作负载优化从吞吐驱动转向延迟受限，亟需超越Tensor Parallelism的延迟导向扩展方法。

Method: 提出CTA-pipelining执行范式，利用共享内存多GPU系统的特性，在Cooperative Thread Array级别建立依赖关系，实现跨GPU的依赖内核并发执行，作为一种延迟导向的空间扩展技术。

Result: 在8-GPU H200和B200系统上，使用CUTLASS、cuBLAS和NCCL库验证，2层GEMM（MLP操作）中CTA-pipelining相比微批处理降低延迟31.8%，相比TP降低29.6%，且可与TP正交组合进一步优化。

Conclusion: CTA-pipelining能有效利用共享内存多GPU系统架构，在延迟敏感场景下显著优于微批处理和TP，并可作为正交扩展维度与TP结合，进一步突破延迟边界。

Abstract: The evolution of compute infrastructure has transformed multi-GPU systems into tightly integrated shared-memory structures. However, current software still mostly treats these coherent interconnects simply as high-speed networks. Simultaneously, the demand for serving Large Language Models under latency constraints has shifted GPU workload optimization from being throughput-driven to latency-bound, necessitating latency-oriented scaling methods beyond Tensor Parallelism (TP).
  Thus, we introduce CTA-pipelining, an execution paradigm designed to exploit shared-memory multi-GPU systems. As a latency-oriented spatial scaling technique, CTA-pipelining leverages dependencies at the Cooperative Thread Array level, enabling concurrent execution of dependent kernels across GPUs. We demonstrate its capability using CUTLASS, cuBLAS, and NCCL libraries on 8-GPU H200 and B200 systems. Results show on 2-layer GEMM, representing the MLP operation, CTA-pipelining reduces latency by up to 31.8% compared to micro-batching, and 29.6% compared to TP. It can also be combined with TP as an orthogonal scaling dimension to further push the latency boundary.

</details>


### [95] [Toward a Unified GPU-Aware OpenSHMEM Specification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08006)
*Naveen Ravi,Nathan Wichmann,Md. Wasi-ur- Rahman,Aurelien Bouteiller,Yıltan Hassan Temuçin,Avinash Kethineedi,Johnathan Alsop,Brandon Potter,Shubhendra Pal Singhal,Jun Shirako,Akihiro Hayashi,Vivek Sarkar,Lawrence C. Stewart,Michael Beebe,Benjamin Michalowicz,Jeongnim Kim,Thiago Teixeria,Mark F. Brown,Aaron Welch,Oscar Hernandez,Wendy Poole,Steve Poole*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出OpenSHMEM GPU感知通信辅助规范，通过最小化内存模型扩展、能力查询和明确定义的语义，为多厂商GPU提供轻量级、向后兼容的PGAS编程模型统一方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GPU启用的OpenSHMEM实现在内存管理、能力发现和操作语义上存在差异，限制了可移植性和生态系统凝聚力，可能导致OpenSHMEM原本要统一的社区分裂。

Method: 提出OpenSHMEM GPU感知通信辅助规范：引入基于GPU作用域内存空间抽象的最小化内存模型扩展，提供能力查询接口，为RMA、原子操作、同步和集合操作中GPU附加缓冲区的使用定义明确语义。该规范通过主机发起接口视角构思，同时提供通用语义支持可选的设备发起操作。

Result: 该规范可在多厂商GPU上实现，为在厂商中立规范下实现统一提供了实用且可快速实现的步骤，同时为未来OpenSHMEM规范设计提供参考。

Conclusion: 通过轻量级向后兼容的辅助规范扩展，可以在不破坏现有生态的前提下，为异构加速器架构下的PGAS模型提供统一的GPU感知通信语义，避免社区分裂。

Abstract: Leadership-class HPC systems are now accelerator-centric, with GPUs providing most floating-point throughput and memory bandwidth. As next-generation systems increasingly integrate accelerators through high-speed memory fabrics and system interconnects, exposing larger tightly coupled device domains, \ac{PGAS} models such as OpenSHMEM provide a natural abstraction for expressing fine-grained remote memory operations across these devices. While OpenSHMEM 1.x offers a lean PGAS model for irregular communication, atomics, fine-grained synchronization, and collectives, its memory model lacks portable semantics for accelerator architectures. As a result, existing GPU-enabled OpenSHMEM implementations differ in memory management, capability discovery, and operation semantics, limiting portability and ecosystem cohesion. This risks fracturing the community that OpenSHMEM was originally created to unify.
  This paper proposes an OpenSHMEM Auxiliary Specification for GPU-Aware Communication, designed as a lightweight, backward-compatible extension to OpenSHMEM 1.x. The auxiliary specification introduces a minimal memory model extension via a GPU-scoped memory space abstraction, along with capability queries and well-defined semantics for using \acs{GPU}-attached buffers in RMA, atomic, synchronization, and collective operations. This is initially conceived through the lens of a host-initiated interface, although it provides a general set of semantics that also allow for optional device-initiated support. A central goal of this effort is to demonstrate that GPU-aware OpenSHMEM semantics can be specified and implemented across GPUs from multiple vendors, providing a practical and rapidly implementable step toward unification under a vendor-neutral specification while informing the design of future OpenSHMEM specifications.

</details>


### [96] [On the Limitations of Non-GPU AI Accelerators for Large-Model Inference: A Field Study of MoE and Multimodal Serving on Huawei Ascend](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08215)
*Zheng Yu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文对华为Ascend 910系统上部署两个大型推理工作负载进行了实地研究，记录了将CUDA迁移至非GPU AI加速器（CANN/vLLM-Ascend）时所需的12个源码级补丁、8类平台限制及操作保障措施，并量化了集成工作量与基准测试质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非GPU AI加速器正被广泛用于大模型推理，但将高要求工作负载从CUDA迁移到这些平台的实际工程成本缺乏充分记录，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 在16设备华为Ascend 910系统上，使用CANN和vLLM-Ascend部署两个工作负载（LLM-as-a-judge安全对齐评估管道和医学视觉语言基准），通过源码级补丁、禁用高吞吐特性、添加操作保障等手段实现可靠性，并系统归类平台限制。

Result: 总结了8类平台限制：算子与功能支持不完整、并行机制脆弱、底层内核数值故障、图编译不成熟、高级功能不稳定、可扩展性有限、可观测性弱、生态碎片化；报告了各限制的症状、证据和可能原因，并量化了集成工作量、并发行为和基准质量。

Conclusion: 本研究为评估或使用非GPU加速器进行大模型推理的团队提供了可复现的参考，揭示了迁移过程中的实际工程挑战和修复策略。

Abstract: Non-GPU AI accelerators are increasingly adopted as alternatives to general-purpose GPUs for large-model inference, but the real engineering cost of migrating demanding workloads beyond CUDA remains poorly documented. We present a field study of deploying two large inference workloads on a 16-device Huawei Ascend 910 system using CANN and vLLM-Ascend: an LLM-as-a-judge safety and alignment evaluation pipeline based on a W8A8 MoE judge model, DeepSeek-V4-Flash, and a multimodal medical vision--language benchmark based on DeepSeek-V4-Flash-Vision for MMMU and MMMU-Pro. Making these workloads reliable required twelve source-level patches to the vendor inference plugin, disabling several high-throughput features to preserve numerical correctness, and adding operational safeguards for recurring device-level failures. We summarize the main platform limitations in eight categories: incomplete operator and feature support, fragile parallelism, numerical faults in low-level kernels, immature graph compilation, unstable advanced features, limited scalability, weak observability, and ecosystem fragmentation. For each category, we report the symptoms, evidence, and likely causes. We also quantify the integration effort, concurrency behavior, and benchmark quality to show that both workloads were served correctly. Our study provides a reproducible reference for teams evaluating or operating non-GPU accelerators for large-model inference.

</details>


### [97] [Self-Stabilizing Algorithms in the Uniform Port Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08244)
*Liam Brinker,Yuval Emek,Oren Louidor*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为“统一端口模型”的真正统一的分布式计算模型，每个图的端口（半边缘）都运行一个恒定大小的有限自动机。在该模型下，首次设计出针对最大独立集、最大匹配、无源定向和最大节点/边k-着色等局部对称破缺问题的、运行于一般图上的高效自稳定算法（多对数时间复杂度）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的真正统一的分布式计算模型以节点为中心，难以自然表述半边缘标签问题（如最大匹配和无源定向）。本文旨在提出一种支持（半）边缘输入/输出标签分配的统一端口模型，填补这一表达力空白，并探索在该模型下是否存在高效的局部对称破缺自稳定算法。

Method: 提出了统一端口模型，每个端口关联一个恒定大小的局部自动机，实现真正的统一性。在该模型下，设计了针对一般图的多对数运行时间的自稳定算法，解决最大独立集、最大匹配、无源定向和最大节点/边k-着色问题。

Result: 首次在真正统一的分布式模型中，证明了存在高效（多对数时间复杂度）的自稳定算法，能够解决多个基本的局部对称破缺问题。

Conclusion: 统一端口模型扩展了真正统一分布式计算的表达力，能够自然地表述半边缘标签问题，并且支持高效的自稳定算法设计，为分布式计算中的局部对称破缺问题提供了新的理论框架。

Abstract: We introduce a distributed computational model referred to as the \emph{uniform port} model. An algorithm operating in this model is defined by means of local automata associated with the ports (a.k.a.\ half-edges) of the input graph. The crux of the uniform port model is that a single constant-size finite automaton is hosted by every port of every graph, making the model \emph{truly uniform}. Moreover, since the new model explicitly supports the assignment of (input and) output labels to the graph's (half-)edges, it facilitates natural formulations of (half-)edge-labeling problems such as maximal matching and sinkless orientation, which are outside the expressivity scope of prior node-centric truly uniform distributed computational models.
  The main technical contribution of this paper is the design of efficient (i.e., with poly-logarithmic runtime) \emph{self-stabilizing} uniform port algorithms, operating on general graphs, for various fundamental local symmetry breaking problems, including maximal independent set, maximal matching, sinkless orientation, and maximal node/edge $k$-coloring. While efficient self-stabilizing algorithms for local symmetry breaking problems have been extensively studied in stronger computational models, our work is the first to demonstrate the existence of such algorithms in a truly uniform model.

</details>


### [98] [Empirical Analysis of GPU Frequency Behavior Under ML Workloads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08307)
*Truong-Thanh Le,Hoang-Loc La,Amir Taherkordi,Frank Eliassen,Phuong Hoai Ha,Peiyuan Guan*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究了NVIDIA GPU在执行ML/AI工作负载时的频率缩放行为，发现低性能GPU的工作频率会受最近约80ms窗口内的工作负载历史显著影响，这导致GPU内核延迟之间存在相互依赖关系，挑战了现有延迟预测技术中将内核延迟视为独立变量的假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ML延迟预测技术普遍假设单个GPU内核延迟是独立的，通过累加独立测量的内核延迟来估算总执行时间。本研究旨在验证这一假设是否成立，并揭示GPU动态频率缩放对延迟预测的影响。

Method: 通过实验观测NVIDIA GPU在执行ML/AI工作负载时的频率缩放行为，分析工作负载历史对GPU运行频率的影响，特别是在低性能GPU上的表现。

Result: 低性能GPU的工作频率受最近约80ms窗口内的工作负载历史显著影响，GPU动态频率缩放引入了内核间的依赖关系，使得独立延迟假设不成立。

Conclusion: 现有ML延迟预测技术中关于内核延迟独立性的假设不一定成立，未来的研究方向包括改进延迟预测模型、GPU内核重排序策略以及面向频率/延迟/能耗感知的模型设计的NAS指导原则。

Abstract: This work presents ongoing research on the frequency scaling behavior of NVIDIA GPUs when executing ML/AI workloads. Our preliminary findings show that, on lower-performance GPUs, the operating frequency is strongly affected by the recent workload history, typically within an 80ms window. This behavior challenges a common assumption underlying several state-of-the-art ML latency-prediction techniques, which treat individual GPU kernel latencies as independent and therefore estimate total execution time by summing isolated per-kernel measurements. Our results indicate that such an assumption does not always hold, as the GPU's dynamic frequency scaling introduces inter-kernel dependencies. We also outline several promising directions for leveraging this observation in future work, including improved latency-prediction models, GPU kernel-reordering strategies, and NAS-driven guidelines for frequency/latency/energy-aware model design.

</details>


### [99] [Adaptive Row Selection Meets Asynchrony in Randomized Kaczmarz](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08313)
*Evan Coleman*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了异步共享内存环境下自适应行选择的随机Kaczmarz算法，通过96核节点上的339次实验，揭示了四个关键实践发现：采样激进性与线程数存在稳定边界、阈值贪婪选择在高线程数下发散、欠松弛可恢复稳定性、不一致读取比一致快照读取更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自适应行选择可减少随机Kaczmarz迭代次数，但部署到共享内存机器时，无锁工作线程并发修改残差并使用过时数据，此前缺乏对此场景的系统性研究。

Method: 在96核节点上运行339次实验，比较残差加权Kaczmarz和贪婪Kaczmarz在异步执行下的表现，使用实际延迟而非注入延迟。

Result: (i) 存在稳定边界ℓ*(T)；(ii) 阈值贪婪选择在高线程数下立即发散；(iii) 欠松弛可在可预测成本下恢复稳定；(iv) 一致快照读取存在罕见的调度相关发散，而不一致读取从未出现且成本更低。

Conclusion: 不一致读取应作为默认选择；研究结果为分布式两级采样器提供了设计依据。

Abstract: Randomized Kaczmarz is a natural fit for large sparse least-squares and tomographic reconstruction, and adaptive row selection can reduce iteration counts. However, deploying adaptive selection on a shared-memory machine means sampling from a residual that lock-free workers are concurrently modifying, often using stale data. We present the first systematic study of this regime: residual-weighted and greedy Kaczmarz under asynchronous execution, measured across 339 runs on a 96-core node with realized (not injected) delays. Four findings carry directly to practice. (i) Stability is governed by a boundary $\ell^*(T)$ between sampling aggressiveness and thread count; below it, more aggressive sampling is strictly better, so one should tune to just inside the cliff. (ii) Threshold-greedy selection (the standard accelerated rule) is unstable at high thread counts, diverging almost immediately. (iii) Under-relaxation buys back the cliff at a predictable cost, giving a usable safety knob. (iv) Consistent-snapshot reads admit a rare, scheduling-dependent divergence that live (inconsistent) reads never exhibited and that is also cheaper, making inconsistent reads the right default. We validate the implementation against published sequential results and outline the distributed two-level sampler these measurements motivate.

</details>


### [100] [Computing in Anonymous Dynamic Networks with One-Bit Communications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08358)
*Thibaut Blanc,Giuseppe Antonio Di Luna,Giovanni Viglietta*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究匿名动态网络中仅靠单比特广播的确定性计算，证明即使每个智能体每轮只广播1比特（仅能获知邻居广播各比特值的计数），仍能实现与拥塞模型（O(log n)比特消息）几乎相同的计算能力：在有领导者和已知上界U时，可在O(n³ log² n + U)轮内终止计算任何可计算的多重集函数；无先验知识时，可在O(n³ log² n)轮内实现自稳定计算。下界证明该复杂度几乎最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索匿名动态网络中计算能力的最基本极限：当通信被极致压缩到每轮每节点仅1比特且只能获得邻居计数时，全局计算是否仍然可能？这揭示了计算所需的根本性信息量下限。

Method: 1) 从局部单比特聚合观测中提取全局线性方程组；2) 设计单比特割测试来获得不可区分智能体类别的守恒约束；3) 通过迭代细化类别并收集独立约束来恢复多重集信息；4) 对未知网络规模引入自适应自校正泛洪原语。

Result: 有领导者且已知U：O(n³ log² n + U)轮终止算法；无先验知识：O(n³ log² n)轮自稳定算法；下界Ω(n² log(N/n)/log n)轮，当N=2^{Ω(n log n)}时变为Ω(n³)。

Conclusion: 匿名动态网络中即使每消息压缩到1比特，其计算能力几乎完全保留，与拥塞模型（O(log n)比特消息）基本匹配。单比特模型几乎达到了信息论意义上的极限。

Abstract: We initiate the study of deterministic computation in anonymous dynamic networks where each agent broadcasts one bit per round and receives only the number of neighbors broadcasting each bit value. Despite this severe restriction, surprisingly rich global computation is possible.
  With a unique leader and a known upper bound $U$ on the network size $n$, we give a terminating algorithm for any computable function of the input multiset in $O(n^3\log^2 n+U)$ rounds, for inputs from a universe of size $N=2^{O(n\log n)}$. Without prior knowledge of $n$, we design a stabilizing algorithm for the same task running in $O(n^3\log^2 n)$ rounds. This essentially matches the state of the art for the congested model, where messages carry $O(\log n)$ bits and general computation takes $O(n^3)$ rounds. We also obtain comparable results for leaderless and multi-leader networks.
  We complement the upper bounds with an almost-matching lower bound of $$Ω!\left(\frac{n^2\log(N/n)}{\log n}\right)$$ rounds, which becomes $Ω(n^3)$ for $N=2^{Ω(n\log n)}$. The proof is information-theoretic, based on local histories, and holds even with a unique leader, known $n$ and $N$, and a communication graph restricted to a dynamically changing ring.
  Our algorithms extract global linear equations from local one-bit aggregate observations. A one-bit cut test yields conservation constraints on the sizes of indistinguishable agent classes; by refining these classes and collecting independent constraints, agents recover the required multiplicities. For unknown size, we introduce a self-correcting adaptive flooding primitive of independent interest. Thus, the computational power of congested anonymous dynamic networks is essentially preserved even when every message is compressed to one bit.

</details>


### [101] [Coded Task Offloading for Fluid Computing: A Privacy-Aware Approach under D2D Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08440)
*Diego Cajaraville-Aboy,Manuel Fernández-Veiga,Ana Fernández-Vilas,Rebeca P. Díaz-Redondo*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于D2D网络的编码任务卸载方案，结合线性秘密共享和任务卸载技术，在随机任务到达和队列动态条件下，联合优化时延、能量和隐私泄露，实现阈值恢复、拖后腿缓解和隐私保护。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有任务卸载方案很少考虑对抗性执行环境下的隐私风险和信息泄漏；大多数编码计算方案专注于缓解拖后腿问题，而未考虑能耗等系统级目标。

Method: 将任务卸载与线性秘密共享方案结合，将任务编码为冗余份额以支持阈值恢复、拖后腿缓解和隐私保护；构建隐私感知卸载问题，在噪声泄漏观测下联合优化时延和能量，并对编码任务的理论隐私泄露进行惩罚；使用分支定界求解器和轻量级启发式调度器求解。

Result: 编码卸载相比经典的全卸载和并行卸载方案改善了时延-能量权衡；启发式算法达到近最优性能，优于基线和最先进的求解器；隐私泄露惩罚会重塑卸载决策，暴露了固有的时延-能量-隐私权衡。

Conclusion: 所提方案在动态异构环境中有效平衡了时延、能耗和隐私保护三个相互冲突的目标，为D2D网络下的隐私感知任务卸载提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Fluid Computing aims to support distributed applications execution across heterogeneous cloud, edge, and device resources, motivating task execution mechanisms that adapt to dynamic and privacy-sensitive environments under runtime conditions. In this context, current task offloading schemes rarely address privacy risks and information leakage under adversarial execution settings; furthermore, most coded computing proposals focus on straggler mitigation without considering system-level objectives such as energy awareness. This paper proposes a coded task offloading scheme for D2D networks under stochastic task arrivals and queue-based dynamics. The proposal combines task offloading techniques with linear secret sharing schemes, where tasks are encoded into redundant shares to support threshold-based recovery, straggler mitigation, and privacy preservation while enhancing system performance. Then, we formulate a privacy-aware offloading problem that jointly optimizes delay and energy while penalizing the theoretical privacy leakage of coded tasks under noisy leakage observations. The problem is solved using a branch-and-bound solver alongside a lightweight heuristic scheduler, both of which are evaluated through a discrete-event simulator. Results show that coded offloading improves the delay--energy trade-off with respect to classical full and parallel offloading schemes, while the heuristic achieves near-optimal performance, outperforming baseline and state-of-the-art solvers. The results also show how privacy leakage penalties reshape offloading decisions, exposing an inherent delay--energy--privacy trade-off.

</details>


### [102] [SMetric: Rethink LLM Scheduling for Serving Agents with Balanced Session-centric Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08565)
*Jiahao Wang,Kaizhan Lin,Kaixi Zhang,Jinbo Han,Xingda Wei,Sijie Shen,Chenguang Fang,Wenyuan Yu,Rong Chen,Haibo Chen*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文针对代理场景下的LLM请求调度问题，提出基于会话的平衡调度方法SMETRIC。通过将每个会话的首次请求专用于负载均衡、后续请求采用缓存感知路由，在保持高KV$复用率的同时提升集群吞吐量10-34%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM调度器针对人类用户设计，不适应代理（Agent）场景下的工作负载特征：代理仅在获得完整响应后行动，因此更关注集群的每秒令牌数（TPS）而非逐令牌延迟；同时请求间KV$复用率高达80%以上（聊天场景仅54-62%）。现有调度器过度优先将请求路由到已缓存KV$的实例，导致部分实例过载而其他实例空闲，限制了TPS。

Method: 提出SMETRIC调度器，核心思想为平衡的会话中心调度（balanced session-centric scheduling）：将每个Agent会话的首次请求纯用于负载均衡（不优先考虑缓存），后续请求采用缓存感知方式路由。利用会话轮次信息作为调度度量，该信息可仅从用户输入高效准确获取，使调度器保持无状态。

Result: 在预填充-解码共部署场景下提升集群TPS 10-16%，在分离架构下提升预填充TPS 2-34%，同时获得更优的逐令牌延迟。

Conclusion: 利用Agent工作负载的会话内局部性，平衡少量请求（会话首个请求）即可实现集群负载均衡，同时不损失大部分本地实例上的KV$复用。全局KV$存储的存在使得负载均衡无需牺牲全部复用机会。

Abstract: LLM scheduling is critical to serving, yet it remains unclear how well existing designs fit agentic serving--with LLM requests issued by agents instead of humans. This shifts the workload in two ways: (1) agents act only on complete responses, making the cluster's tokens per second (TPS) the primary goal and relaxing--not eliminating--per-token latency requirements; and (2) requests share much of their KV\$-reuse exceeds 80% of request tokens in a production trace from BAILIAN, versus 54-62% in chat.
  This paper first contributes a systematic study of request scheduling for agents on two real-world traces. We find that to increase KV\$ reuse, existing schedulers overly prioritize routing requests to instances caching their KV\$, overloading a few while leaving the rest idle, capping TPS. We thus present two key insights: (1) load balance need not sacrifice all KV\$ reuse, thanks to the global-tier KV\$ store and (2) by utilizing the workload's intra-session locality, balancing a small fraction of requests--the first request in each agent session--suffices to balance the cluster without sacrificing most KV\$ reuse on local instances.
  SMETRIC realizes these insights with balanced session-centric scheduling: it routes each session's first request purely for load balance and its follow-up requests in a cache-aware manner, preserving load balance and local reuse while keeping demand on the global tier low. Using the session turn information as the scheduling metric is deliberate: it is derived efficiently and accurately from the user inputs alone, so the scheduler stays clean and stateless. SMETRIC improves cluster TPS by 10-16% under prefill-decode colocation with a global store and prefill TPS by 2-34% under disaggregation over state-of-the-art schedulers, also with a better per-token latency.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [103] [Context Graphs for Proactive Enterprise Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07721)
*Avinash Kumar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出基于Context Graph的主动式智能代理框架，通过持续监控企业实体状态变化、计算主动性评分并利用LLM生成排名通知，将信息 surfaced 时间从47分钟（反应式基线）降至30秒以下，Precision@5达0.83，假阳性率0.11。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG和代理系统本质上是"反应式"的，必须等待人工查询才能行动，无法在用户提问之前主动推送相关信息，限制了企业生产力的提升。

Method: 构建Context Graph（实时关系数据结构，建模企业实体、关系及状态变迁）；Delta Detection Engine（持续监控状态变化）；Proactivity Scorer（基于紧迫性、相关性和角色匹配度排名候选洞察）；Surfacing Layer（LLM驱动，生成带可解释性说明的排名通知）。给出统一的主动性评分函数及完整Python实现。

Result: 在合同生命周期管理、工程事件响应和销售管道卫生三个企业案例中，Precision@5为0.83，假阳性率0.11，平均信息 surfaced 时间从47分钟降至30秒以下。

Conclusion: 基于上下文图谱的主动式代理显著优于传统反应式基线，能有效提升企业生产力。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) and agentic frameworks have advanced enterprise AI considerably, yet agents remain fundamentally reactive: they wait for a human query before acting. This paper argues that genuine enterprise productivity gains require proactive agents: systems that surface relevant, actionable information to workers before they ask. We propose the Context Graph, a live relational data structure that models enterprise entities, their relationships, and state transitions over time. Built on this graph, we define a Delta Detection Engine that continuously monitors state changes, a Proactivity Scorer that ranks candidate insights by urgency, relevance, and persona-fit, and a Surfacing Layer powered by an LLM that delivers ranked notifications with grounded explanations. We formalize each component, derive a unified Proactivity Score function, and provide a complete end-to-end Python implementation using NetworkX and the Anthropic Claude API. Evaluation across three generic enterprise case studies (contract lifecycle management, engineering incident response, and sales pipeline hygiene) demonstrates that context-graph-driven proactivity achieves Precision@5 of 0.83, a false positive rate of 0.11, and reduces mean time to surface from 47 minutes (reactive baseline) to under 30 second.

</details>


### [104] [AI-integrated models for assessing agricultural resilience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07759)
*Joshua R. Waite,Dana Golden,Brett Indelicato,Kevin Camp,Mojdeh Saadati,Shannon Regan,Patrick Schnable,Baskar Ganapathysubramanian,Carlos Messina,Suzanne Thornsbury,Soumik Sarkar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文开发了一个AI驱动的工具，将经济模型（GTAP）与生物物理模型（APSIM）集成，用于分析农业供应链冲击，允许政策制定者和市场参与者通过自然语言查询和响应来评估跨学科影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 农业供应链容易受到生物物理和经济系统耦合的中断影响，现有工具难以跨学科评估这些冲击，需要一种集成方法。

Method: 将经济可计算一般均衡模型（GTAP）与农业生物物理模型（APSIM）集成，并利用AI技术实现自然语言交互界面。

Result: 开发了一个支持自然语言查询和响应的AI工具，能够整合经济和生物物理模型来分析供应链冲击。

Conclusion: 该工具使政策制定者和市场参与者能够通过自然语言交互评估跨学科影响，增强对农业供应链中断的理解和应对能力。

Abstract: Agricultural supply chains are vulnerable to disruptions through linked biophysical and economic systems. We develop an AI-powered tool that integrates economic models (GTAP) with biophysical models (APSIM) to analyze supply chain shocks, enabling policymakers and market participants to assess cross-disciplinary impacts through queries and responses written in natural language.

</details>


### [105] [Adversarial Social Epistemology for Assemblies of Humans and Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07760)
*Mihnea C. Moldoveanu,Joel A. C. Baum*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种对抗性社会认识论（ASE），用于分析在密集交互的传播环境中，代理如何利用证言链、推理、制度认证和隐性信任来扭曲、隐瞒或伪造信息，并提供了审计和补救信任 breaches 的机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有概念（如认知气泡、回音室、错误信息传播）不足以解释在由证言链、推理和制度认证支撑的密集传播环境中，代理如何利用本应使这些断言值得信赖的承诺和权利来故意扭曲信息。

Method: 提出了对抗性社会认识论（ASE）框架，结合认识论网络和推理主义语义学，分析了代理如何破坏可审计性链条，并提供了审计和补救信任 breach 的机制。

Result: 提供了描述代理如何利用社会认识论承诺和权利的分析语言，概述了破坏信任的机制，并建立了审计和补救的框架。

Conclusion: 对抗性社会认识论能够更好地解释复杂传播环境中的信息扭曲现象，并为建立可审计的公共传播系统提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We outline an adversarial social epistemology (ASE) for densely interactive communicative landscapes in which public assertions are scaffolded by chains of testimony, inference, institutional certification, and tacit trust. In such landscapes, agents have incentives and affordances to distort, color, omit, fabricate, or strategically under-specify information for private, reputational, rhetorical, or material gains. We argue that these phenomena are not adequately captured by familiar descriptions of epistemic bubbles, echo chambers, or misinformation diffusion. What requires explanation is how communicative agents exploit the commitments and entitlements that normally make scaffolded assertions trustworthy. We provide language that delivers the requisite analysis, outline mechanisms that subvert trust in scaffolded public communications, and outline machinery for auditing and redressing trust breaches arising from subverting the auditability of inferential chains, drawing on epistemic networks, enriched with an inferentialist semantics for interpreting assertions.

</details>


### [106] [Aligning Clinical Needs and AI Capabilities: A Survey on LLMs for Medical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07761)
*Qi Peng,Jiatong Li,Sirui Huang,Yiyang Jiang,Kaisong Gong,Ronger Ding,Shijie Ye,Changmeng Zheng,Yi Cai,Xiaobo Yang,Jin Huang,Xiao-Yong Wei,Qing Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该综述论文采用临床-计算双视角方法，基于Miller金字塔建立了五级医疗能力框架，并将演绎、归纳、溯因推理模式与医疗任务对应，通过包含五级推理能力的基准数据集对18个最新模型进行评估，发现医疗专科模型在诊断任务中表现优异，而通用模型在决策支持和对话方面领先，最后讨论了数据限制、幻觉和接地等挑战及未来方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型在医疗领域展现出越来越大的潜力，有必要系统性地梳理其在临床推理中的应用现状、能力层级划分以及评估方法，以推动更安全、更可靠的医疗LLM系统发展。

Method: 采用双视角（临床实践与计算方法）分析框架：临床侧基于Miller金字塔建立五级能力体系（从知识回忆到动态病例管理）；计算侧将演绎、归纳、溯因三种推理模式与常见医疗目标和任务关联。同时构建覆盖五级医疗推理能力的基准数据集，并对18个前沿模型进行系统评估。

Result: 医疗专科模型在诊断类任务中表现优异，而通用模型在决策支持和对话类任务上领先。研究揭示了不同模型在不同推理层级和任务类型上的能力差异。

Conclusion: 当前进展表明LLM在医疗推理方面具有潜力但仍面临数据限制、幻觉、接地问题等挑战，未来需要朝着更安全、更可靠、更适配临床工作流的方向发展。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have emerged as important tools in healthcare, showing growing potential for clinical reasoning and patient care. This survey examines recent progress in medical LLMs, focusing on reasoning applications and requirements. We present a dual-view approach that connects clinical practice with computational methods. On the clinical side, we establish a five-level competency scheme following Miller's Pyramid, progressing from knowledge recall to dynamic case management. On the computational side, we link deductive, inductive, and abductive reasoning patterns to common medical goals and tasks. We also introduce a benchmark dataset spanning five levels of medical reasoning capability and report results on 18 state-of-the-art models, revealing that medical specialist models excel in diagnosis-centric tasks while general models lead in decision support and dialogue. We conclude by discussing current progress and open challenges, including data limitations, hallucination, and grounding issues, and outline directions toward safer, more reliable, and workflow-ready systems.

</details>


### [107] [Alignment Plausibility: A New Standard for Assuring AI in Healthcare](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07766)
*Gwydion Williams,Sara Zannone,Bilal A Mateen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出，使大语言模型在心理健康支持方面实现结构性安全，需要模仿人类临床实践的保障机制，在三个层面进行对齐：明确价值观规范、嵌入价值观的训练、以及部署中的监督。作者提出"对齐合理性"（alignment plausibility）这一监管概念，用以系统论证AI系统是否真正对齐于积极的健康结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM已广泛用于心理健康支持，但其作为注意力经济产物，商业目标偏向维持用户参与而非提供有效心理帮助所需的"摩擦"。开发者对安全问题的反应多是被动应对最显性的急性伤害，忽视了依赖性、边界侵蚀、扭曲信念放大等长期隐性的风险模式。

Method: 借鉴人类临床实践的三层安全保障体系（规范承诺→培训嵌入→临床监督），提出LLM对齐的三层结构：(1)基于临床实践规范的显式价值观设定；(2)将价值观嵌入模型的训练；(3)部署中检测漂移和长期伤害的监督机制。由此构造"对齐合理性"概念，类比生物学合理性（biological plausibility）作为AI健康的监管框架。

Result: 提出了"对齐合理性"这一结构化论证框架，用以展示系统的价值观、训练机制和监督机制共同与安全、积极的结果保持一致。该框架可作为监管工具，用于论证系统是否值得信任：是否对齐积极健康结果、是否即使有能力造成伤害也不会造成伤害、以及是否最终惠及患者。

Conclusion: 将AI对齐组织为三层结构（规范→训练→监督）并引入"对齐合理性"概念，为心理健康领域的LLM安全提供了一种类似临床实践保障的、可论证的监管框架，弥补当前仅被动应对显性伤害的安全范式之不足。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have become significant providers of mental health support, yet they remain products of an attention economy whose operational and commercial targets favour sustained engagement over the friction that effective psychological support often requires. Developers' safety responses have been largely reactive, addressing the most visible and acute harms while subtler, longer-term patterns of risk (e.g., dependency, boundary erosion, the amplification of distorted beliefs) receive less attention. We contend that making LLMs structurally safe requires alignment organised at three levels that mirror how society assures the safety of human clinical practice: 1) explicit value specification grounded in the codified normative commitments of clinical practice; 2) training that embeds those values in the model; and 3) oversight that detects drift and longer-term harm during deployment, much as clinical supervision does for human practice.
